### [1] [Native Multi-Dimensional Subquadratic Operators via Input Dependent Long Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19378)
*David R. Wessels,Farhad Ramezanghorbani,David W. Romero,Alireza Moradzadeh,Olivia Viessmann,Maksim Zhdanov,John St. John,Ken Janik,David M Knigge,Yucheng Tang,Erik J Bekkers,Saee Gopal Paliwal*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: HyenaND是一种亚二次、全局、输入依赖的多维算子，通过隐式参数化的全局多维卷积核直接操作数据原生几何结构，并利用CUDA实现FFT卷积融合，在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像和PDE建模中匹配或超越注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有亚二次注意力替代方案（如标准卷积和递归模型）在处理多维数据时存在局限：卷积缺乏全局感受野和输入依赖性，递归模型需将图像/体积/PDE光栅化为1D扫描顺序，破坏空间结构。

Method: 提出HyenaND，通过隐式参数化全局、输入依赖的多维卷积核，直接在数据原生几何结构上执行卷积运算，并结合FFT-卷积融合的CUDA实现nSubQ，将复杂度O(L log L)转化为实际加速。

Result: 纯HyenaND堆叠在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像和PDE建模中匹配强注意力基线的准确率；混合配置（HyenaND与注意力层交错）超越纯注意力和基于递归的强混合模型。

Conclusion: HyenaND作为多维数据的亚二次全局算子，在保持高效性的同时无需折衷空间结构，为注意力机制提供可行替代，且混合架构进一步提升了性能。

Abstract: Subquadratic alternatives to attention require compromises when applied to multi-dimensional data: standard convolutions lack global receptive fields and input dependency, while recurrent models require rasterizing data such as images, volumes, and partial differential equation (PDE) into an ad-hoc $1\rm D$ scan order that violates their spatial structure. We introduce \textit{HyenaND}, a subquadratic, global, input-dependent operator that acts directly on the native geometry of multidimensional data through convolutions with implicitly parametrized global, input-dependent multi-dimensional convolutional kernels. Our CUDA implementation, \texttt{nSubQ}, fuses the FFT-convolution path to turn HyenaND's $\mathcal{O}(L \log L)$ scaling into wall-clock speedups. Across long-context genomics, computer vision, medical imaging, and PDE modeling, pure HyenaND stacks match the accuracy of strong attention baselines, while hybrid configurations that interleave HyenaND and attention layers outperform both pure attention and strong recurrence-based hybrids.

</details>


### [2] [Bayesian Wind Tunnels for Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19379)
*Siddhartha R Dalal,Vishal Misra,Abhay Parekh*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文通过构建贝叶斯风洞环境，证明小型Transformer能在无先验结构假设下实现精确贝叶斯模型选择，并揭示算术感知条件是关键瓶颈：模型在整数token上成功，但在透明符号上彻底失败，且该界限在模型扩大112倍后依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Transformer能否超越固定假设类内的贝叶斯滤波，实现更通用的贝叶斯模型选择——即从数据中识别正确的假设类（如对合与3-循环），并探究其能力边界。

Method: 设计可控的贝叶斯风洞，采用固定点自由对合（$f(f(x))=x$）作为纯关系任务，比较整数token与透明符号两种编码，通过类后验熵、MAE等指标评估Transformer与贝叶斯最优解的匹配度。

Result: 2.8M参数Transformer在整数token上实现0.01-bit熵匹配，在非嵌套比较（对合vs3-循环）中MAE低于0.001；但算术感知任务（模加法/乘法）中，整数token成功而透明符号失败，且该差距在316M参数模型上仍存在；固定重标定的透明符号可恢复成功。

Conclusion: Transformer能进行类贝叶斯模型选择，但严重依赖算术感知的稳定语义；透明符号的失败源于无法编译电路而非注意力头解析本身，前沿LLM存在约55倍的校准差距，表明该能力在不同尺度上具有非线性。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that transformers can perform exact Bayesian filtering within a fixed
  hypothesis class. Can they also perform Bayesian model selection -- identifying the correct
  hypothesis class from data? We introduce model-selection Bayesian wind tunnels: controlled
  environments where ground-truth posteriors over hypothesis classes are available in closed
  form. Using fixed-point-free involutions -- whose defining property f(f(x))=x is purely
  relational -- a 2.8M-parameter transformer achieves 0.01-bit entropy agreement with the
  Bayesian optimum (3 seeds), with both integer tokens and opaque symbols whose meanings
  change every episode. This extends to non-nested comparisons: involutions vs. 3-cycles
  (where neither class is a subset of the other) achieve class-posterior MAE under 0.001,
  demonstrating genuine model selection beyond simplicity/subset bias. We then identify a
  sharp perceptual access condition: when the discriminative statistic requires arithmetic --
  modular addition (rotations) or multiplication (f(x)=cx mod p) -- model selection succeeds
  with integer tokens but fails completely with opaque symbols, and this boundary persists
  under 112x scaling (2.8M to 316M parameters). A stationarity control confirms the operative
  factor: opaque tokens with a fixed relabeling succeed (0.009-bit MAE), showing that stable
  semantics, not integer identity, enable circuit compilation. Header subtask diagnostics
  localize the failure to the composition of header inversion with arithmetic rather than
  header parsing itself. Probing frontier LLMs on the same tasks shows qualitative Bayesian
  behavior but a large calibration gap (~55x), measured through lossy probes and therefore
  directional rather than exact.

</details>


### [3] [Air Quality Arena: A Large-Scale Multi-Region Ground Monitoring Dataset and Benchmark for Air Quality Forecasting with Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19381)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Manik Gupta,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出涵盖7国、6种污染物、超14000个站点序列的大规模空气质量数据集与基准AQA，评估11种时间序列基础模型在短期预测上的表现，发现跨模态视觉基础模型取得了最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空气质量预测基准存在地理范围窄、污染物种类少、缺乏对最新时间序列基础模型的评估。

Method: 构建包含多国多污染物的大规模数据集AQA-Data，设计标准化评估流程AQA- Bench，对11种时间序列基础模型和经典基线进行零样本短期预测性能比较。

Result: 时间序列基础模型在零样本场景下持续优于经典基线，其中采用跨模态架构（结合视觉基础模型）的模型表现最佳。

Conclusion: AQA提供了更全面的空气质量预测基准，验证了时间序列基础模型在大规模实际数据上的零样本预测有效性。复现代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Air pollution causes an estimated 7.9 million premature deaths annually, making accurate forecasting a critical public health priority. Machine learning is increasingly being applied to forecast air pollution levels, yet existing benchmarks remain narrow in both geographic scope and pollutant coverage, and fail to evaluate the latest generation of time series foundation models (TSFMs) on real world, large scale data. We present Air Quality Arena (AQA), a large scale multi-country and multi-pollutant dataset (AQA-Data) and benchmark (AQA-Bench) to address this gap. AQA covers 6 major pollutants over a three year period across 7 diverse countries and 4 continents, with more than 14,000 station-pollutant series, aiming to provide a comprehensive benchmark for air quality tasks. We benchmark this dataset across 11 leading time series foundation models and classical baselines to assess performance on short-term air quality forecasting. Our results demonstrate that TSFMs are effective zero-shot forecasters and consistently outperform classical baselines, with our top-performing model employing a cross-modal architecture that leverages a vision foundation model for time series forecasting. AQA is publicly released at AirQualityArena.github.io

</details>


### [4] [Scale-Aware Learning of Chaotic Dynamics on Unstructured Meshes via Binned Spectral Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19387)
*Kanad Sen,Romit Maulik*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出基于图拉普拉斯频段的谱损失函数，用于非结构化网格上非线性动力系统的替代建模，通过可扩展的切比雪夫滤波器和多层级GLEAM方法提升长期推演保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化网格上的频带谱损失无法直接用于不规则网格，缺乏规范傅里叶基，需构建基于图算子的谱表示来监督混沌系统的尺度结构。

Method: 用图拉普拉斯频段替代傅里叶频段，采用切比雪夫多项式图滤波器近似精确谱分解实现可扩展性，并提出GLEAM方法在多层级图架构中对粗细表示进行尺度感知正则化。

Result: 在非结构化网格湍流预测任务中，所提谱损失相比确定性基线提升了长期推演保真度，并保持了统计不变量。

Conclusion: 图拉普拉斯频段谱损失有效适用于非结构化网格的混沌系统替代建模，可扩展近似方法在保持性能的同时降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Surrogate modeling for high-dimensional nonlinear dynamical systems that exhibit chaos requires mechanisms that preserve not only pointwise accuracy but also the scale-dependent structure of physical fields. Bandwise spectral power losses, such as the binned spectral loss function, provide such supervision on structured grids, where Fourier modes define a standard frequency decomposition. On irregular meshes, however, no canonical Fourier basis exists, and spectral representations must be constructed from graph operators induced by mesh connectivity and geometry. In this study, we extend the binned spectral power loss for application to unstructured-mesh surrogate modeling of nonlinear dynamical systems. This is obtained by replacing Fourier bands with graph-Laplacian frequency bands, and we provide scalable Chebyshev and multilevel approximations for improving long-horizon rollout fidelity. In its full-spectrum form, our approach uses graph Laplacian eigenspaces to provide a graph analogue of Fourier band-power matching, but incurs the high cost of spectral decomposition. As a scalable approximation, we replace exact band projectors with sparse Chebyshev polynomial graph filters, avoiding explicit eigendecomposition. When utilizing multilevel graph architectures, we introduce Graph Laplacian Energy Alignment for Meshes (GLEAM), which applies retained-subspace scale-aware supervision across graph hierarchies so that coarse and fine representations are regularized during autoregressive rollout. Our results show that the proposed spectral losses improve long-horizon rollout fidelity and preserve statistical invariants for the forecasting of turbulent flows on unstructured meshes, compared to deterministic baselines.

</details>


### [5] [Predicting Groundwater Arsenic Concentrations Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19392)
*William Xing,Stephanie Yang,Aarush Bandemegal,Anushree Misra,Ananya Kalapatapu,Brennan Lagasse,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究构建了整合美国74,000多个砷样本的空间数据集，评估了多种机器学习模型，发现图神经网络能利用空间依赖性匹配或超越梯度提升树的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国地下水砷污染构成长期公共健康危机，特别是依赖私人井的家庭，但缺乏可泛化的模型来预测砷浓度的空间连续变化。

Method: 将砷预测视为回归任务，通过k近邻和GIS技术整合来自水质门户、矿产资源数据系统和网格化国家土壤调查地理数据库的砷测量点，并评估了基于树的集成方法、多层感知器和图神经网络。

Result: 梯度提升树在表格数据中仍为最先进方法，但图神经网络能进一步考虑空间依赖性，匹配或超越梯度提升树的结果。

Conclusion: 基于图的空间感知学习能增强环境预测，为改进地下水风险绘图和监测提供基础。

Abstract: Arsenic contamination in groundwater presents a longstanding public health crisis in the United States, especially for households depending on private wells. Accurate and spatially informed prediction of arsenic concentration is vital to identify high-risk areas and focus mitigation efforts. However, there is a lack of generalizable models for representing continuous variation in arsenic concentrations across regions. In this work, we pose arsenic prediction as a regression task and construct a spatially integrated dataset to aggregate over 74,000 arsenic samples from the Water Quality Portal (WQP), Mineral Resources Data System (MRDS), and Gridded National Soil Survey Geographic Database (gNATSGO). Specifically, we use a variety of techniques including kNearest Neighbors (k-NN) and Geographic Information Systems (GIS) to join arsenic measurement points from across the United States by location. Building on this dataset, we evaluate a diverse suite of machine learning models, including tree-based ensemble approaches, multilayer perceptrons, and spatially aware graph neural networks (GNN). Our findings show that while gradient-boosted trees are still considered state-of-the-art in the field of tabular data, GNNs are able to further account for spatial dependence to match or outperform the results of gradient-boosted trees. These results demonstrate that graph-based and spatially informed learning can enhance environmental prediction and provide a foundation for improved groundwater risk mapping and monitoring.

</details>


### [6] [When Does Consensus Beat Voting? A Critical Analysis of Statistical Label Fusion in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19402)
*Renjie He*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文严格论证了共识分割的数学基础，发现STAPLE算法在常见条件下退化为阈值多数投票、存在95%的EM次优性且易在类别不平衡下崩溃，而多数投票和深度共识模型更优，且共形预测可提供形式化不确定性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示STAPLE等复杂共识分割方法在实际医学影像中的严重缺陷，纠正领域内对“高级方法”的过度依赖。

Method: 从生成模型、EM算法、边缘化分析、可辨识性条件、Spatial STAPLE到深度变分公式的逐层理论推导，并通过控制实验验证预测。

Result: STAPLE在典型场景下退化为多数投票、EM收敛至局部次优的概率高达95%、类别不平衡时完全失效；多数投票作为非参数基线表现稳健；深度共识模型联合图像与标签使问题可解；共形预测实现实用不确定性保证。

Conclusion: 应批判性评估共识方法而非默认使用STAPLE，多数投票是强大的基准，深度模型和共形预测提供了更原则性的替代方向。

Abstract: This paper provides a rigorous, self-contained investigation of consensus segmentation. We derive the mathematical foundations from first principles -- the generative model, EM algorithm, Van Leemput's marginalization analysis, identifiability conditions, Spatial STAPLE, and deep variational formulations -- and validate each theoretical prediction through controlled experiments. The central finding is sobering: under common conditions, STAPLE reduces to thresholded majority voting, suffers 95% EM suboptimality, and collapses under class imbalance. These are not edge cases but typical scenarios in medical imaging. Majority voting -- simple, non-parametric, and robust -- is a surprisingly strong baseline that the field has perhaps too hastily dismissed in favor of more "sophisticated" methods. At the same time, the deep consensus model demonstrates that the consensus problem is not inherently difficult -- it becomes tractable when the image is used alongside the labels. And conformal prediction shows that formal uncertainty guarantees are achievable and practical. We hope this work encourages practitioners to critically evaluate their consensus methods rather than applying STAPLE by default, and provides the mathematical and empirical foundation for more principled approaches.

</details>


### [7] [Marine Engine Fault Dataset: Open-Access Data under Controlled Reference and Fault Scenario Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19444)
*Ahmad BahooToroody,Oleksiy Bondarenko,Mohammad Mahdi Abaei,Niki Yoichi,Enrico Zio*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个公开的船用柴油机故障数据集，包含正常运行和五种人为引入的异常工况的多传感器时间序列数据，可用于故障诊断和状态监测研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开的船用发动机预测性维护数据集稀缺，尤其是那些包含受控故障实验、记录运行条件、子系统干预和系统层面测量的数据集。

Method: 在试验台上对一台涡轮增压、中冷的三缸船用柴油机进行参考性能测试（30-90%负载）和故障场景测试，通过物理干预引入冷却水泵气蚀、空滤堵塞、空气冷却器结垢、喷油嘴堵塞和涡轮退化五类异常，并记录多传感器时间序列数据。

Result: 参考测量值在运行范围内保持物理一致性，人为异常产生可解释的响应模式，且不同严重程度的异常行为逐渐可区分。

Conclusion: 该数据集为船用机械的异常检测、故障诊断、退化建模等状态监测研究提供了经过充分记录的基准。

Abstract: Open-access datasets for marine-engine predictive maintenance remain scarce, particularly those from controlled fault experiments with documented operating conditions, subsystem-level interventions and system-level measurements. This work presents the Marine Engine Fault Dataset, an openly available dataset from a turbocharged, intercooled three-cylinder marine diesel engine operated on a testbed under both reference and fault-scenario conditions. The experimental campaign combined a reference-performance program across the 30-90% load range with scenario-based tests in which abnormal conditions were introduced after stabilized fault-free operation, enabling controlled comparison between baseline and fault-affected behaviour. Five anomaly classes were implemented through physical interventions affecting major engine subsystems: cooling-water pump cavitation, compressor air-filter clogging, air-cooler fouling, injection-valve nozzle clogging and turbine degradation induced through increased exhaust-side restriction. The released data comprise multi-sensor time-series of operating, thermal, pressure, flow and combustion-related variables, with a separate reference-performance record and metadata for structured reuse. Technical validation shows that the reference measurements remain physically coherent across the operating range and that the imposed anomalies produce interpretable response patterns consistent with the affected subsystems, including progressively distinguishable behaviour where different severities were implemented. By combining controlled fault realization, multi-load operation and system-level measurements within a real marine-engine platform, the dataset provides a well-documented benchmark for anomaly detection, fault diagnosis, degradation modelling and related condition-monitoring studies in maritime machinery.

</details>


### [8] [Geospatial Diffusion-based Evolution Synthesis (GeoDES) for Storm-Centered Weather Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19522)
*Sonia Cromp,Satya Sai Srinath Namburi GNVV,Youran Wang,Grace Kisslinger,Frederic Sala,James Booth,Allegra LeGrande*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: GeoDES是一种基于图像到视频扩散的模型，用于生成高保真、物理一致的大尺度天气事件（如气旋风暴），以弥补机器学习天气模型在精细结构预测上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气模型难以预测气旋风暴等大尺度天气系统的详细结构：区域模型受限于固定地理边界的历史记录，全球模型计算昂贵且分辨率不足，无法捕捉细粒度风暴动力学。

Method: 提出GeoDES（地理空间扩散进化合成模型），这是一种定制的图像转视频扩散模型，严格聚焦于风暴结构的演变生成，以合成物理一致的高保真天气事件。

Result: 在北大西洋测试集上，GeoDES比次优方法峰值涡度误差降低52%，异常相关系数提高8%，显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: GeoDES能有效生成高质量、物理一致的风暴演进序列，可用于天气预报模型的压力测试和气象数据集的扩展。

Abstract: While machine learning-based weather models hold significant promise, they struggle to predict the detailed structure of large-scale weather systems such as cyclonic storms. Regional models are constrained by limited historical records within fixed geographic boundaries, while global models are computationally expensive and often operate at resolutions too coarse to capture fine-grained storm dynamics. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Geospatial Diffusion-based Evolution Synthesis (GeoDES) model, a custom image-to-video diffusion model. By focusing generation strictly on the evolving storm structure, GeoDES synthesizes physically consistent, high-fidelity weather events suitable for stress-testing forecast models and expanding meteorological datasets. Evaluations demonstrate that GeoDES outperforms prior methods on key metrics, achieving $52\%$ lower Peak Vorticity Error and $8\%$ higher Anomaly Correlation Coefficient than the next strongest methods on the North Atlantic test set.

</details>


### [9] [Symbol and Footprint Database for Electronic Components by Agentic Recognition and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19767)
*Yichen Shi,Yuzhi Liu,Zhuofu Tao,Li Huang,Yuhao Gao,Ting-Jung Lin,Lei Hel*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SFgen，利用多模态大语言模型自动生成电子元件的符号和封装，构建SFnet数据库以支持PCB自动设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCB设计中手动创建符号和封装耗时且易错，亟需自动化方法提高效率与准确性。

Method: 基于多模态大语言模型开发智能体识别与生成流程SFgen，实现符号和封装的自动生成。

Result: 符号生成准确率86%，封装生成准确率80%，构建含1000个组件且持续扩展的SFnet数据库。

Abstract: A rich and recognizable component library is the cornerstone of printed circuit board (PCB) design and generation. Traditionally, engineers manually create symbols and footprints and design PCB schematics, which is time-consuming and error-prone. Leveraging multimodal large language models (MLLMs), we develop SFgen, an agentic recognition and generation flow of symbol and footprint for electronic components. SFgen achieves 86% accuracy for symbol generation and 80% accuracy for footprint generation. We use the SFgen method to create SFnet, a database of symbols and footprints. It now has 1000 components and is expanding constantly, which lays the foundation for automatic generation of PCB designs.

</details>


### [10] [The C-index illusion: discrimination without calibration in published survival models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19526)
*Rafael da Silva,Danilo Alvares*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文复现了三个非临床领域的生存分析模型，发现仅用C-index评估模型会导致对校准性和时间依赖准确性的系统性忽视，五个预注册假设中有三个被拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锚点论文在合成数据上论证了仅用C-index评估生存模型会产生误导，但这一发现是否适用于真实世界的非临床模型尚未被检验。

Method: 在硬盘故障、P2P信贷违约和数字平台用户流失三个领域复现已发表的生存-ML模型，使用锚点论文的合成实验验证评估工具，并在Holm校正的族系错误率下测试五个预注册假设。

Result: 三个假设被拒绝：一个模型C-index接近报告值但校准检验失败（p=2.6e-136）；将贷款提前还款视为非信息删失会导致风险高估约2个百分点；平台流失模型的概率估计随预测时间退化而全局C-index仍正常。直接检验指标选择是否反转模型偏好未被拒绝，但功效有限。

Conclusion: 在真实场景中，仅依赖C-index评估生存模型会导致对模型性能的过度自信，而非完全选错模型；校准性和时间依赖性应被纳入评估体系。

Abstract: "Stop Chasing the C-index when Evaluating Survival Analysis Models" (ICML 2026, Spotlight) argued normatively, on synthetic data, that evaluating survival models by discrimination alone, i.e. the concordance index, produces systematically misleading model comparisons, because the metric ignores calibration and time-dependent accuracy. Whether this matters for real, published, non-clinical models has not been tested. We reproduce three published survival-ML models across three structurally distinct domains (hard-drive failure, peer-to-peer credit default, and user disengagement on digital platforms), validate our evaluation instrument against the anchor paper's own synthetic experiment, and test five pre-registered hypotheses under a Holm-corrected family-wise error rate. Three of five reject. A model that reproduces the published discrimination almost exactly (C = 0.9595 vs. 0.958 reported) fails a formal calibration test at p = 2.6e-136; a broad feature-ablation search finds no single attribute responsible for this discrimination, so the calibration failure is not an artifact of a trivial shortcut. A lender's estimated default risk is biased upward by roughly two percentage points, growing to nearly four points in the riskiest segment, when loan prepayment is treated as non-informative censoring rather than as a competing risk. A platform's churn model shows probability estimates that degrade with the prediction horizon even as its global discrimination stays within the pre-registered C-index band. A direct test of whether metric choice inverts which model is preferred does not reject, though with limited power given only two to three models per domain; the failure mode we document is better characterized as misplaced confidence in a chosen model than as choice of the wrong one. We release a reusable, pre-registered evaluation harness with full code and a single annotated notebook.

</details>


### [11] [Silent Failures in Multimodal Agentic Search:A Diagnostic Taxonomy and Cross-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19793)
*Zhengxian Wu,Junjie Gao,Kai Yang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了多模态智能搜索中隐蔽失败的六类分类法，并构建了轨迹级诊断流程，实验表明表面准确性高估了真实轨迹正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注最终答案准确性，可能遗漏搜索轨迹中的失败行为。

Method: 基于六类分类法（模态捷径、幻觉定位、错误证据正确答案、过度检索、跨模态矛盾、出处幻觉），在统一的ReAct框架下构建轨迹级诊断流程，评估答案正确性与证据质量。

Result: 在四种前沿多模态模型上的实验表明，表面准确性一致高估真实轨迹级正确性；交叉验证、空白图像压力测试等进一步显示隐蔽失败具有能力依赖性且常转移而非消失。

Conclusion: 现有表面准确性指标不足以反映多模态智能搜索的可靠性，需要关注轨迹级隐蔽失败。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic search systems increasingly rely on external tools to answer knowledge-intensive visual questions. However, existing evaluations mainly focus on final-answer accuracy and may miss failures in the search trajectory. In this work, we study such hidden reliability issues as silent failures. We introduce a six-category taxonomy covering modality shortcuts, phantom grounding, wrong-evidence-right-answer cases, over-retrieval laundering, cross-modal contradiction, and provenance hallucination. Based on this taxonomy, we build a trajectory-level diagnostic pipeline that evaluates both answer correctness and evidence-grounding quality under a unified ReAct-style scaffold. Experiments on MMSearch-Plus trajectories across four frontier multimodal models show that surface accuracy consistently overestimates true trajectory-level correctness. We further use cross-judge validation, blank-image stress tests, and tool ablations to show that silent failures are capability-dependent and often shift rather than disappear. Home-page: https://github.com/DingWu1021/silent-failures-multimodal-agentic-search

</details>


### [12] [DocOps: A Verifiable Benchmark for Autonomous Agents in Complex Document Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19865)
*Jiazhen Jiang,Boxi Cao,Lingyong Yan,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出DocOps框架，对智能体操作数字文档的能力进行确定性验证，发现前沿模型在长程耦合任务中存在严重局限，并揭示了三种关键失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体可靠操作数字文档的能力尚未被系统评估，现有方法缺乏层次化、可验证的评价标准，难以刻画实际工作流中的复杂操作需求。

Method: 构建基于层次化分类法的DocOps评估框架，将文档操作分解为原子维度与递增的流程复杂度，并对闭源和开源模型在不同智能体框架下进行系统测试。

Result: 最先进的智能体配置在高度耦合、长距离任务中表现严重不足；细粒度行为分析发现三种失败模式：长期状态追踪崩溃、浅层语义验证、结构性元数据破坏性编辑。

Conclusion: 揭示了智能体在维护全局文档一致性方面的能力边界，为设计鲁棒、非破坏性的复杂数字生态系统智能体提供了方向。

Abstract: As autonomous agents rapidly evolve, their ability to reliably manipulate ubiquitous digital documents has become critical for enabling general-purpose AI assistants and automating complex workspace workflows. In this paper, we introduce DocOps, a deterministically verifiable evaluation framework underpinned by a hierarchical taxonomy that deconstructs document operations inspired by real-world practices into atomic dimensions and escalating workflow complexities. Based on DocOps, we systematically evaluate representative closed- and open-source models across various agentic harnesses, revealing that even the most advanced frontier configurations still exhibit profound limitations when handling highly coupled, long-range tasks. Furthermore, a fine-grained analysis of existing agents' manipulation behaviors uncovers 3 key failure modes: long-term state tracking collapse, shallow semantic verification, and destructive editing of structural metadata. Ultimately, our work exposes the capability boundaries of agents in maintaining global document consistency, shedding light on the future design of robust, non-destructive agents for complex digital ecosystems.

</details>


### [13] [SenWorld: A Digital-Twin Simulation for Generating Context-Rich Evaluation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19949)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoyang Li,Xiaoxuan Qiao,Zhilang Wei,Tianming Lei*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SenWorld，一种基于数字孪生的确定性仿真框架，生成带有固定真实标注的隐私安全评估数据，用于测试智能手机个人助理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机个人助理需要上下文丰富的评估数据，但真实设备数据因隐私无法共享，现有方法依赖后验标注或大语言模型评审，缺乏可靠的真实标注。

Method: 构建物理化、确定性、事件驱动的数字孪生仿真环境，利用真实地图、天气、节假日和网络数据，让虚拟角色经历完整一天，将所有可观测信号存档为全系统快照，每个评估用例通过指向已有记录而非后验标注来标注。

Result: 在北京16个虚拟角色的实验中，生成数据在类别分布（JSD 0.070）和日常通信节奏（JSD低于0.1）上与真实用户基准高度吻合，但记录较短；虚拟角色形成完全互动的对话子图和差异化行为；717个评估用例发现生产助手78个失败，集中在通话和短信记录，且每个失败通过快照指针确认为检索错误。

Conclusion: SenWorld提供了一种隐私安全、可重复、分布验证的评估数据生成路径，其标注由构造固定，无需大语言模型评审。

Abstract: Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. In SenWorld, personas live through a full day in a world built from real map, weather, holiday, and network data; every observable signal is archived in full-system snapshots; and each evaluation case is labeled by a pointer to an existing record rather than by post-hoc annotation or a large language model (LLM) judge. We evaluate this method with 16 personas in Beijing. The generated data closely matches the held-out real-user benchmark in category distribution (Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) 0.070) and in the daily rhythm of communication records (JSD below 0.1), though generated records remain shorter than real ones. Without scripted interaction, personas form a fully reciprocated dialogue subgraph and differentiated behavioral repertoires. Projected into 717 evaluation cases, the generated data exposes 78 failures in a production smartphone assistant, concentrating on call and Short Message Service (SMS) records while contacts, schedules, and alarms never fail. The snapshot pointer confirms each failure as an assistant-side retrieval error, with no LLM judge involved. Overall, SenWorld offers a privacy-safe, reproducible, and distribution-checked path to evaluation data whose labels are fixed by construction.

</details>


### [14] [Analytic Distribution of Classifier-Free Guidance for Schedule Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19725)
*Enze Jiang,Zheng Ma*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文分析了无分类器指导（CFG）在扩散模型中的采样分布，发现其并不符合常见的乘积分布启发式。通过概率流ODE推导出精确的路径积分表示，揭示了CFG对分布的修正机制，并基于此提出一种时间依赖的指导调度DG-CFG，在Stable Diffusion上提升了生成质量与多样性平衡，同时减少了采样步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CFG机制在条件生成中被广泛使用，但其确定性引导动力学采样的分布并不被通常的乘积分布启发式所描述，缺乏精确的理论理解，导致实际应用中的调度方式往往是启发式的，性能不佳。

Method: 通过概率流ODE对CFG进行分析，推导出常数和时间依赖指导下分布精确的解析路径积分表达式，揭示CFG通过指数路径积分修正扰动分布。基于此，提出Distribution-Guided CFG（DG-CFG）调度，平衡时间步贡献并考虑信号强度和低噪声分数误差放大。

Result: 玩具模型验证了分布预测的准确性。在Stable Diffusion 1.5上，DG-CFG提升了生成质量，实现了更强的多样性-保真度权衡，尤其在强指导导致饱和和质量下降时效果显著。在固定图像质量目标下，DG-CFG减少了采样步数，降低了采样成本。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析揭示了CFG的真实分布修正机制，并基于此提出的DG-CFG调度能有效改善扩散模型的生成效率与质量，为条件生成中的调度设计提供了新的理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is the default mechanism for conditional generation in diffusion models, but the distribution sampled by its deterministic guided dynamics is not captured by the usual product-distribution heuristic $p_0^ωq_0^{1-ω}$. We analyze CFG through the probability flow ODE and derive exact analytic path-integral representations of the induced distributions for both constant and time-dependent guidance. The resulting formulas show that CFG modifies $p_{t_0}$ by an exponential path-integral correction, and that a time-dependent schedule enters this correction through the weight $ω(t)-1$. This characterization explains how score discrepancies accumulate along sampling trajectories and motivates Distribution-Guided CFG (DG-CFG), a schedule that balances timestep contributions while accounting for signal strength and low-noise score-error amplification. A toy model with analytic scores closely verifies the predicted distributions. On Stable Diffusion~1.5, DG-CFG improves generation and yields a stronger diversity--fidelity trade-off across guidance strengths, with especially clear gains when strong guidance causes saturation and quality degradation in constant and heuristic schedules. Across NFE budgets, DG-CFG reaches fixed image-quality targets with fewer sampling steps, reducing the sampling cost needed to achieve target metrics.

</details>


### [15] [EvoDRC: A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Automated DRC Violation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20019)
*Bing-Yue Wu,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出EvoDRC框架，通过技能演化与LLM代理实现DRC自动修复，在DAC26基准测试中平均DRC违规减少73.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRC收敛是先进节点物理设计中的主要瓶颈，现有手动ECO迭代效率低且易引入新违规，亟需自动化修复方法。

Method: 从无关参考设计蒸馏层特定修复技能，通过目标设计修复经验持续演化；将版图分解为有界区域并分配LLM修复代理，结合本地DRC分析、连通性检查和影响预览工具反馈，将修复操作及DRV变化存入知识库以进化技能。

Result: 在DAC26 DRC基准的7个模块级设计中，相比基线平均减少73.5%的DRC违规。

Conclusion: EvoDRC通过知识蒸馏与持续演化实现高效的DRC自动化修复，显著降低违规数量，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Design rule check (DRC) closure remains a major bottleneck in advanced-node physical design. Although detailed routers are rule-aware, residual design rule violations (DRVs) often require manual engineering change order iterations. Automating this process is challenging because repairs must account for complex geometric interactions, preserve circuit connectivity, and avoid introducing new violations. We present EvoDRC, a skill-evolution framework for agentic block-level DRC repair. EvoDRC initializes layer-specific repair skills using knowledge distilled from an unrelated reference design and continuously evolves these skills using traceable repair experience collected from the target design. EvoDRC decomposes the layout into bounded repair regions and assigns an LLM repair agent to each region. Local DRC analysis, connectivity-checking, and impact-preview tools provide feedback on proposed modifications. Repair operations and their resulting DRV changes are stored in a knowledge database and used to evolve the repair skills. Experiments on seven block-level designs from the DAC26 DRC Benchmark show that EvoDRC achieves a 73.5\% overall reduction compared to the reported baseline.

</details>


### [16] [Global Difference Constraint Propagation for Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20022)
*Lucas Kletzander,Jip J. Dekker,Andreas Schutt,Peter J. Stuckey*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全局界限一致性传播器，用于同时处理所有形如 x - y ≤ d 的差分约束，并生成解释以支持惰性子句生成求解器，实验表明其显著优于标准传播方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准有限域传播将每个差分约束视为独立传播器，虽保证完备性但速度慢，而现有差分约束理论求解器不直接满足传播器需求，因此需要一种能全局处理并生成解释的高效传播方法。

Method: 基于SAT模理论中差分约束理论求解器的基础，构建一个全局界限一致性传播器，并设计机制生成传播的解释，以集成到惰性子句生成求解器中。

Result: 实验表明，全局处理差分约束相比标准传播方法能大幅提升性能。

Conclusion: 全局差分约束传播器结合解释生成，可有效加速约束求解，尤其适用于惰性子句生成框架。

Abstract: Difference constraints of the form $x - y \leq d$ are well studied, with efficient algorithms for satisfaction and implication, because of their connection to shortest paths. Finite domain propagation algorithms, however, typically do not make use of these algorithms, and treat each difference constraint as a separate propagator. Propagation does guarantee completeness of solving, but can be needlessly slow. In this paper we describe how to build a (bounds consistent) global propagator for difference constraints that treats them all simultaneously. SAT modulo theory solvers have included theory solvers for difference constraints for some time. While a theory solver for difference constraints gives the basis of a global difference constraint propagator, we show how the requirements on the propagator are quite different. Crucially, we show how to explain propagations by a global difference constraint propagator, in order to use it within a lazy clause generation solver. We give experiments showing that treating difference constraints globally can substantially improve on the standard propagation approach.

</details>


### [17] [AlphaRoute: Large Language Models as Semantic Optimizers for Multi-Objective Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19768)
*Kabir Murjani,Mishri Bhavsar,Manish I. Patel,Jonti Talukdar*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: AlphaRoute是一种将LLM作为语义策略优化器的VLSI全局布线框架，通过SHAP分解和自适应PathFinder策略，在ISPD 2025基准测试中实现最高98.6%的溢出减少和29.8倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统启发式方法使用静态惩罚调度，无法处理复杂拥塞拓扑，而VLSI全局布线是NP-hard问题，需要多目标优化。

Method: 将rip-up and reroute重构为动态优化系统，使用SHAP分解隔离每网拥塞，通过3D Dijkstra迷宫路由和自适应PathFinder策略驱动子图提取，并利用LLM在确定性知识图谱约束下动态调整惩罚参数。

Result: MEMPOOL溢出减少98.6%；ARIANE设计溢出降至146,109（29.8倍减少），惩罚分数0.0538对比SOTA的1.780。

Conclusion: AlphaRoute证明优越的算法搜索几何结构能够克服Python解释实现的延迟，显著超越当前最优方法。

Abstract: Very Large Scale Integration (VLSI) global routing is an NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem requiring signal net assignment across capacity-constrained 3D grids while minimizing congestion, wirelength, and via transitions. Because traditional heuristics rely on static penalty schedules that fail on complex congestion topologies, we present AlphaRoute: a multi-objective adaptive search framework reformulating rip-up and reroute (R&R) into a dynamic optimization system. We introduce SHAP-based overflow decomposition to isolate per-net congestion, driving targeted subgraph extraction via 3D Dijkstra maze routing and an adaptive PathFinder policy. Crucially, AlphaRoute employs Large Language Models (LLMs) as semantic policy optimizers. Bounded by a deterministic knowledge graph, the LLMs interpret congestion metrics to dynamically adjust penalty parameters. Evaluated on ISPD 2025 benchmarks, AlphaRoute reduces overflow by 98.6% on MEMPOOL. On the constrained ARIANE design, we achieve an overflow of 146,109 (a 29.8x reduction in overflow over the state of the art), yielding a penalized score of S_orig = 0.0538 versus the State-of-the-art (SOTA) 1.780. These results demonstrate that superior algorithmic search geometry can overcome the latency of interpreted Python implementations.

</details>


### [18] [Local Causal Structure Learning in the Presence of Latent Variables and Selection Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19866)
*Zheng Li,Hao Zhang,Ruxin Wang,Ruichu Cai,Kun Zhang,Feng Xie*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了LoCaLS算法，能在存在隐变量和选择偏差的情况下，从观测数据中高效且准确地识别目标变量的直接原因和结果，无需学习全局因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局因果学习方法计算成本高，而局部方法常假设没有隐变量和选择偏差，这些假设在实际数据中常被违反。

Method: 先刻画一个局部区域用于目标特定的因果发现，再建立该局部区域观测分布与全局因果结构之间的理论关联，最后提出基于这些理论的LoCaLS算法。

Result: 在随机和真实结构上，LoCaLS比现有局部方法具有更高的结构准确性，同时比全局方法显著节省计算量；在真实基因表达数据中识别出生物学上合理的因果结构。

Conclusion: LoCaLS在允许隐变量和选择偏差的设定下，能以更低的计算代价达到与全局方法相同的直接原因和效果识别效果，适用于大规模生物数据分析。

Abstract: Discovering the direct causes and effects of a target variable from observational data is a fundamental problem in causal discovery, with broad applications in domains such as gene regulatory analysis and biomedical research. Existing causal discovery methods either learn a global causal structure, which incurs substantial computational cost, or assume the absence of latent variables and selection bias, assumptions that are often violated in real-world settings. Motivated by these challenges, we study local causal structure learning in the presence of latent variables and selection bias. Specifically, we first characterize a local region that enables target-specific causal discovery without recovering the entire global structure. We then establish a theoretical bridge between causal information learned from the observed distribution induced on this local region and the corresponding information in the global causal structure. Building on these foundations, we propose LoCaLS, a local causal structure learning algorithm that is sound and complete under standard assumptions and identifies the same direct causes and effects of a target variable as those identifiable by global causal discovery methods, while allowing for latent variables and selection bias. Extensive experiments on random and real-world structures demonstrate that the proposed method consistently achieves higher structural accuracy than existing local methods while requiring substantially less computational effort than state-of-the-art global methods. Furthermore, applications to two real-world gene expression datasets reveal biologically plausible target-specific causal structures, demonstrating its practical applicability in large-scale biological data analysis.

</details>


### [19] [Good Practice Guide for quantifying uncertainties for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19999)
*P. Harris,C. Bench,M. Rinkevičius,V. Marozas,L. Coquelin,A. Thompson,M. Nandi,U. Hackstein,P. J. Aston*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本指南总结了QUMPHY项目中针对光电容积描记（PPG）信号应用的机器学习模型与不确定性量化方法，涵盖模型选择、回归/分类任务、不确定性量化技术实现与验证、六个基准问题及数据集、辅助软件和伦理考量，最终给出建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器学习模型在处理可穿戴设备PPG信号时的不确定性问题，提供系统化的实践指导以提升模型可靠性。

Method: 通过比较不同机器学习模型（回归与分类任务），分类阐述模型依赖型与模型独立型不确定性量化方法，并设计六个基准问题及配套数据集与软件工具。

Result: 形成了一套涵盖模型选择、不确定性量化实施与验证的综合性指南，并提供了基准测试框架与开源软件。

Conclusion: 该指南为PPG信号相关机器学习应用提供了标准化指导，尤其强调了不确定性量化的必要性，并建议在实际部署中结合伦理审查。

Abstract: This Good Practice Guide presents work done in the QUMPHY project (Uncertainty quantification for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals) that considered both machine learning and uncertainty quantification for problems which used photoplethysmography (PPG) signals from wearable devices as input. It provides high-level guidance on what types of machine learning model might be used and how different models compare when applied to both regression and classification tasks. It provides guidance on the implementation of different methods for uncertainty quantification, covering both model-dependent and model-independent techniques, and on the validation of the results provided by those methods. It also describes six benchmark problems together with pointers to different benchmark datasets for each problem. Software is described that can assist practitioners in implementing the methods described herein and there is a brief consideration of ethical issues. It concludes with a summary and recommendations.

</details>


### [20] [CURED: Creating, Understanding, and Repairing Errors Demonstrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20140)

Abstract: Detecting and cleaning errors in tabular data is a prerequisite for data intense software applications. Recent research at the intersection of Machine Learning (ML) and Database Management Systems (DBMS) highlights the potential of statistical learning algorithms for error detection and cleaning. This paper combines our recent work on ML-based data cleaning and error models in a unified demonstrator. The web application allows users to upload tabular data, perturb the data with realistic data dependent errors and use modern ML methods to clean and understand error mechanisms in data. Our demonstrator helps to bridge the gap between theoretical advancements and intuitive practical insights in the context of error models and data cleaning algorithms for tabular data. The demonstrator is available at https://cured.demo.calgo-lab.de/

</details>


### [21] [Instance Hardness-Based Relevance for Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20173)
*Vitor M. Leitao,Juscimara G. Avelino,George D. C. Cavalcanti,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种基于实例硬度的相关函数(InHaR)，用于识别回归问题中的稀有实例，尤其在双峰分布下优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于目标值分布的相关函数在双峰等复杂分布中无法准确区分真正稀有与正常实例。

Method: InHaR通过结合实例的学习难度和目标分布来定义相关性，指导重采样策略如随机过采样和高斯噪声。

Result: InHaR正确识别双峰分布下的稀有区域，并在重采样中显著提升预测性能。

Conclusion: InHaR是一种有效的稀有实例识别方法，为不平衡回归提供了改进的解决路径。

Abstract: Imbalanced regression problems arise when the target variable has an asymmetric distribution, resulting in underrepresented value ranges in the dataset. Traditional approaches for identifying rare instances rely on a relevance function that assigns higher importance to specific regions of the target distribution. However, the effectiveness of imbalance-aware learning methods depends strongly on how relevance is defined. In more complex scenarios, such as bimodal distributions, traditional relevance functions struggle to capture rarity, as they assign fixed relevance values based solely on target values, thereby compromising the distinction between truly rare and normal instances. To address these limitations, this study proposes an Instance Hardness-based relevance function (InHaR) for identifying rare instances in regression problems. Unlike traditional relevance functions, the proposed approach incorporates learning difficulty, allowing rarity to be inferred not only from the target distribution but also from the difficulty of instances for the learning algorithm. This property is particularly important in bimodal scenarios, where rarity cannot be accurately inferred from target values alone. Experimental results demonstrate that the InHaR correctly identifies rare regions under bimodal distributions and, when used to guide resampling strategies such as Random Oversampling (RO) and Gaussian Noise (GN), leads to significant improvements in predictive performance compared to traditional relevance-based approaches. The code, dataset, and further details about the proposed method are publicly available at https://github.com/VitorLeitao/instance-hardness-Imbalanced-regression.

</details>


### [22] [User-Centric Modeling of Transactional Sequences with Explainable State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20228)
*Ivan Palagin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出结合对比表示学习与状态空间模型的混合方法，通过两种整合策略提升用户建模性能，在三个数据集上验证了效果，收敛速度提升2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习(CoLES)能生成高质量用户表示，但现有编码器(如RNN、Transformer)存在梯度消失或二次复杂度问题；Mamba虽能高效处理长程依赖，但在个性化用户分析中尚未充分探索。

Method: 两种整合策略：将CoLES嵌入初始化Mamba隐藏状态，或将投影后的CoLES嵌入作为前缀标记拼接到输入序列，使模型从第一步即获得用户先验。

Result: 在三种数据集(年龄预测、多标签产品获取、二元购买预测)上，混合模型一致优于纯Mamba和CoLES+线性分类器，收敛速度比基线快2-3倍。

Conclusion: 混合方法有效结合对比学习和状态空间模型的优势，提升了用户建模性能与训练效率，可解释性分析揭示了选择性事件过滤行为。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid approach for user-centric modeling of transactional event sequences that combines contrastive representation learning (CoLES) with State Space Models (SSMs). While contrastive methods yield high-quality compressed user representations, existing encoders -- RNNs and Transformers -- suffer from vanishing gradients or quadratic complexity, respectively. Mamba, a selective SSM, efficiently handles long-range dependencies but remains underexplored for personalized user analysis. We investigate two integration strategies: (1)~initializing the Mamba hidden state with a CoLES embedding, and (2)~prepending the projected CoLES embedding as a prefix token to the input sequence. Both approaches supply the model with an informative user prior from the first step. Experiments on three public datasets -- Age (multiclass age-group prediction), MBD (multi-label product acquisition), and Taobao (binary purchase prediction) -- demonstrate consistent improvements over standalone Mamba and CoLES with a linear classifier, with the hybrid models converging 2--3$\times$ faster than the plain SSM baseline. Explainability analysis via discretization-step maps and Integrated Gradients reveals selective event filtering on behavior-rich datasets and identifies the most informative transaction features.

</details>


### [23] [Breaking the $T^{3/4}$ Barrier for Regret Minimization With Bi-Dimensional CDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20258)
*Matteo Castiglioni,Anna Lunghi,Alberto Marchesi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 针对形式为 g(x)·P(X≤x) 的 CDF 相关目标函数，在[0,1]^2上提出了一种新的在线学习算法，将遗憾界从之前的 Õ(T^{3/4}) 改进至 Õ(T^{7/10})，并应用于重复双边贸易中的利润最大化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法因维度诅咒导致遗憾上界与下界之间存在 Õ(T^{3/4}) 与 Ω(T^{2/3}) 的差距，需要探索能否通过特定目标结构部分消除该诅咒。

Method: 设计了一种结合多臂老虎机与在线凸优化思想的算法，利用 Lipschitz 函数的已知性以及二元反馈的统计特性，通过自适应网格划分和置信区间上界策略实现遗憾最小化。

Result: 在假设 g 为已知 Lipschitz 函数且分布未知的条件下，算法取得了 Õ(T^{7/10}) 的遗憾上界，优于先前 Õ(T^{3/4}) 的界，且该界在重复双边贸易的固定价格设置中得到验证。

Conclusion: 证明了对于这类 CDF 相关目标，维度诅咒可被部分克服，但 Õ(T^{7/10}) 与 Ω(T^{2/3}) 的差距表明仍有改进空间。

Abstract: We study regret minimization for learning CDF-related objectives of the form \[ g(x)\cdot\mathbb{P}_{X\sim\mathcal{D}}(X\le x), \] over $[0,1]^2$, where $g$ is a known Lipschitz function and $\mathcal{D}$ is an unknown distribution. At each round $t$, the learner selects a point $x_t$ and observes the binary feedback $\mathbb{I}(X_t\le x_t)$, where $X_t\sim\mathcal{D}$. We design an algorithm achieving regret $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$, improving over the previous best-known bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})$ and showing that the curse of dimensionality can be at least partially lifted for this class of objectives, though a gap remains with the $Ω(T^{2/3})$ lower bound. As an application, our techniques yield the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$ regret bound for profit maximization in repeated bilateral trade with fixed prices.

</details>


### [24] [Multi-modal transformer for signal classification in nanopore blockade experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20323)
*Sandro Kuppel,Julian Hoßbach,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态深度学习架构，联合处理原始时间序列、小波图像和静态特征向量，在42种肽和20种氨基酸数据集上分别提升准确率超过10%和接近完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米孔信号复杂，可靠分配信号给特定分子是主要挑战。

Method: 构建多模态深度学习模型，同时分析三种信号表示：原始时间序列、小波变换图像和静态特征向量，并通过注意力机制整合互补信息。

Result: 在42种肽基准上准确率超现有方法10个百分点以上，迁移至20种氨基酸数据集时达到近完美准确率。

Conclusion: 多模态深度学习能实现基于纳米孔传感器的高鲁棒性、高精度分子识别。

Abstract: Nanopore devices have emerged as powerful tools for single-molecule sensing, with potential for rapid, portable diagnostics. They detect changes in ionic current as analytes enter nanometer-scale pores, providing a means of identifying diverse biomarkers from their characteristic signal patterns. However, these signals are highly complex, and reliably assigning them to specific molecules remains a major challenge. Here, we address this by introducing a multi-modal deep learning architecture that jointly processes multiple signal representations, including raw time-series data, wavelet-based images, and static feature vectors. Our approach surpasses existing methods by more than 10 percentage points on a 42-peptide benchmark and transfers to a 20-amino-acid dataset with near-perfect accuracy. The model integrates complementary information from these representations, with attention analysis showing that the time-series and wavelet-image inputs emphasize different features of the same event. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of machine learning to enable robust, high-accuracy molecular identification with nanopore sensors.

</details>


### [25] [Online Variance Reduction for Domain Adaptation on Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20374)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出首个在线随机方差缩减算法ARROW，用于流式数据的MMD和CORAL损失，通过自适应重加权最小批次实现方差缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线SVR算法不适用于在线、分布式或增量学习场景，需要在线方差缩减方法。

Method: 维护对齐统计量的移动平均参考，自适应重加权输入最小批次使其与参考统计对齐，并采用松弛重加权方案使权重优化问题可解。

Result: 在实验和模拟中，ARROW在运行时间、方差缩减程度和目标域准确度上与离线算法竞争。

Conclusion: ARROW是首个有效的在线SVR算法，适用于流式数据的MMD和CORAL损失，性能与离线算法相当。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of stochastic variance reduction (SVR) for the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) and correlation alignment (CORAL) loss functions. Although various offline SVR algorithms for these losses have been proposed, these are incompatible with online, distributed, or incremental learning settings. This paper presents Adaptive vaRiance Reduction via Online reWeighting (ARROW), the first online SVR algorithm for the MMD and CORAL for streamed data. The method maintains moving average references of the alignment statistics, and adaptively reweights incoming minibatches so that the minibatch and reference statistics are aligned. Further, we propose a relaxed reweighting scheme so that the ensuing weight-optimisation problem is tractable. In experiments and simulations, we show that ARROW performs competitively with offline algorithms in terms of runtime, degree of variance reduction achieved, and target domain accuracy.

</details>
